6499    //    favour of either. Picking the signed one would silently ignore what
6500    //    the caller asked; picking the unsigned one would let a parameter
6501    //    override a signature.
6502    if let (Some(hint_client), Some(param_client)) =
6503        (hint_client_id.as_deref(), q.client_id.as_deref())
6504        && hint_client != param_client
6505    {
6506        return build_oauth2_error_response(&OAuth2Error::InvalidRequest(
6507            "id_token_hint and client_id identify different clients".into(),
6508        ));
6509    }
6510
6511    let effective_client_id = hint_client_id.or_else(|| q.client_id.clone());
6512
6513    // 3. End the session, and notify the clients that were in it.
6514    let (Some(session_id), Some(subject_id)) = (session_id, subject_id) else {
6515        // T23.1.8 / P23W1-10 — no signed statement of which session: the one
6516        // this browser's OP cookie names is the only candidate, and only the
6517        // `/logout` sub-path of the authorization endpoint can read it.
6518        return bounce_to_cookie_logout(
6519            &req,
6520            tenant_id,
6521            &EndSessionContinuation {
6522                tenant_id: Some(tenant_id),
6523                post_logout_redirect_uri: q.post_logout_redirect_uri,
6524                state: q.state,
6525                client_id: effective_client_id,
6526            },
6527        );
6528    };
6529    dispatch_backchannel_logout(
6530        &state,
6531        tenant_id,
6532        session_id,
6533        subject_id,
6534        tenant_issuer.as_deref(),
6535    )
6536    .await;
6537    // A session that has already expired is not an error — `invalidate` and
6538    // the refresh-token revocation are both idempotent, so the user asked to
6539    // be logged out and they are. A *failed* write is different (D-N39,
6540    // #839): the session or the refresh tokens issued under it may still be
6541    // live, and redirecting to the relying party as if the logout had worked
6542    // would leave it half-ended without anyone being told.
6543    if state
6544        .auth_service
6545        .logout(tenant_id, session_id)
6546        .await
6547        .is_err()
6548    {
6549        // The repeat names the session through the same hint, so the
6550        // OP-session cookie is not needed for it.
6551        return logout_incomplete_page(&state.auth_config, tenant_id, false);
6552    }
6553
6554    // 4. Redirect against the identified client's allow-list, or render.
6555    finish_logout(
6556        &state,
6557        tenant_id,
6558        effective_client_id.as_deref(),
6559        q.post_logout_redirect_uri.as_deref(),
6560        q.state.as_deref(),
6561    )
6562    .await
6563}
6564
6565/// What [`end_session`] hands to its cookie-reading `/logout` hop (T23.1.8).
6566///
6567/// Exactly the parameters `end_session` would itself have used after deciding
6568/// that no signed hint named a session — and nothing else. In particular no
6569/// `id_token_hint`: the hop is the cookie's arm, never a second place a signed
6570/// hint is interpreted. Every field is as untrusted here as it was there, and
6571/// is validated the same way.
6572#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, utoipa::IntoParams)]
6573pub struct EndSessionContinuation {
6574    /// The tenant. Required on the deployment-wide path; on a per-tenant path
6575    /// it is the path segment, and `TenantPathScope` supplies it (a client
6576    /// that sends it there is refused, as on every tenant path).
6577    pub tenant_id: Option<Uuid>,
6578    /// Honoured only on the identified client's allow-list, by exact match —
6579    /// the `end_session` rule.
6580    pub post_logout_redirect_uri: Option<String>,
6581    /// Echoed verbatim on a redirect that actually happens. Never interpreted.
6582    pub state: Option<String>,
6583    /// Chooses the redirect allow-list; never identifies a session.
6584    pub client_id: Option<String>,
6585}
6586
6587/// The `302` from `end_session` to the `/logout` sub-path of the authorization
6588/// endpoint the request arrived under (T23.1.8, P23W1-10).
6589///
6590/// Same-origin and by path, built from the typed tenant on a tenant path and
6591/// from the constant otherwise — never from anything the request carried. On a
6592/// tenant path the continuation carries no `tenant_id` (the scope would refuse
6593/// it); on the deployment-wide path it must. No cookie is set here: see
6594/// [`end_session`].
6595fn bounce_to_cookie_logout(
6596    req: &HttpRequest,
6597    tenant_id: Uuid,
6598    continuation: &EndSessionContinuation,
6599) -> HttpResponse {
6600    let on_tenant_path = crate::middleware::tenant_path::binding_of(req).is_some();
6601    let path = format!("{}/logout", authorize_path_of(req));
6602    let mut query = url::form_urlencoded::Serializer::new(String::new());
6603    if !on_tenant_path {
6604        query.append_pair("tenant_id", &tenant_id.to_string());
6605    }
6606    if let Some(uri) = continuation.post_logout_redirect_uri.as_deref() {
6607        query.append_pair("post_logout_redirect_uri", uri);
6608    }
6609    if let Some(st) = continuation.state.as_deref() {
6610        query.append_pair("state", st);
6611    }
6612    if let Some(client_id) = continuation.client_id.as_deref() {
6613        query.append_pair("client_id", client_id);
6614    }
6615    let query = query.finish();
6616    let location = if query.is_empty() {
6617        path
6618    } else {
6619        format!("{path}?{query}")
6620    };
6621    HttpResponse::Found()
6622        .append_header((actix_web::http::header::LOCATION, location))
6623        // The URL carries the relying party's `state` and its post-logout
6624        // target; it must not be cached or travel on as a referrer.
6625        .append_header((actix_web::http::header::CACHE_CONTROL, "no-store"))
6626        .append_header(("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer"))
6627        .finish()
6628}
6629
6630/// `GET /oauth2/authorize/logout` (and `/t/{tenant_id}/oauth2/authorize/logout`)
6631/// — the cookie-reading half of RP-Initiated Logout (T23.1.8, P23W1-10).
6632///
6633/// Reached by `end_session`'s `302` when no `id_token_hint` named a session.
6634/// It sits *under* the authorization endpoint's path for one reason: RFC 6265
6635/// §5.1.4 path-match sends the `axiam_op_session` copy scoped to that path
6636/// here, and to nowhere else a logout could be handled. So this is the one
6637/// place a logout without a signed hint can find out which session the browser
6638/// holds.
6639///
6640/// What it does, in order:
6641///
6642/// 1. reads the cookie and looks its digest up **in the tenant the request
6643///    names** — the same tenant-keyed read `/oauth2/authorize` makes, so a
6644///    copy minted in tenant A names nothing here when the request names B;
6645/// 2. revokes that one row, and nothing else — no back-channel fan-out (see
6646///    `end_session`'s "No confirmation prompt");
6647/// 3. clears every OP-cookie copy for the tenant, and the API cookies;
6648/// 4. continues exactly as `end_session` would have: an allow-listed
6649///    `post_logout_redirect_uri` by exact match, or AXIAM's own page.
6650///
6651/// GET only, as a navigation target; public (a logout must work for a session
6652/// that is already gone); rate-limited with the `end_session` preset under its
6653/// own bucket. Reached directly rather than through `end_session` it does
6654/// nothing `end_session` does not already do for the same navigation — it
6655/// clears this browser's cookies and ends the session those cookies named.
6656#[utoipa::path(
6657    get,
6658    path = "/oauth2/authorize/logout",
6659    tag = "oauth2",
6660    params(EndSessionContinuation),
6661    responses(
6662        (status = 302, description = "The OP browser session this browser's cookie named \
6663                                      is ended; redirected to an allow-listed \
6664                                      post_logout_redirect_uri"),
6665        (status = 200, description = "The OP browser session this browser's cookie named \
6666                                      is ended; AXIAM's logged-out page"),
6667        (status = 400, description = "No tenant could be determined"),
6668        (status = 500, description = "The session could not be fully ended and is still \
6669                                      live; no redirect. The API cookies are cleared and the \
6670                                      OP browser-session cookie is kept, so repeating the \
6671                                      request completes the logout"),
6672    ),
6673)]
6674pub async fn end_session_at_cookie_path<C: Connection + Clone>(
6675    req: HttpRequest,
6676    query: web::Query<EndSessionContinuation>,
6677    state: web::Data<AppState<C>>,
6678) -> HttpResponse {
6679    let q = query.into_inner();
6680    let Some(tenant_id) = q.tenant_id else {
6681        return build_oauth2_error_response(&OAuth2Error::InvalidRequest(
6682            "tenant_id is required".into(),
6683        ));
6684    };
6685
6686    if let Some(cookie) = req.cookie(crate::middleware::csrf::COOKIE_OP_SESSION) {
6687        let digest = axiam_auth::token::hash_browser_session_token(cookie.value());
6688        match state
6689            .session_repo
6690            .get_by_browser_token_hash(tenant_id, &digest)
6691            .await
6692        {
6693            Ok(Some(session)) => {
6694                // As in `end_session`: an already-ended session is a logout
6695                // that succeeded, and a failed write (D-N39) is not. The
6696                // session is still live after a failure (F4-R12-07) and this
6697                // cookie is what names it, so it is kept for the retry.
6698                if state
6699                    .auth_service
6700                    .logout(tenant_id, session.id)
6701                    .await
6702                    .is_err()
6703                {
6704                    return logout_incomplete_page(&state.auth_config, tenant_id, true);
6705                }
6706                tracing::info!(
6707                    %tenant_id,
6708                    session_id = %session.id,
6709                    "RP-initiated logout without an id_token_hint sid ended the session \
6710                     the browser's OP cookie named"
6711                );
6712            }
6713            Ok(None) => {}
6714            // Logged without the cookie value or its digest, as at the
6715            // authorization endpoint.
6716            Err(e) => tracing::warn!(
6717                error = %e,
6718                %tenant_id,
6719                "could not resolve the OP browser session at logout; clearing the \
6720                 cookies regardless"
6721            ),
6722        }
6723    }
6724
6725    finish_logout(
6726        &state,
6727        tenant_id,
6728        q.client_id.as_deref(),
6729        q.post_logout_redirect_uri.as_deref(),
6730        q.state.as_deref(),
6731    )
6732    .await
6733}
6734
6735/// The common tail of every RP-initiated logout: resolve the redirect against
6736/// the identified client's allow-list, and clear every cookie either way.
6737async fn finish_logout<C: Connection + Clone>(
6738    state: &web::Data<AppState<C>>,
6739    tenant_id: Uuid,
6740    client_id: Option<&str>,
6741    post_logout_redirect_uri: Option<&str>,
6742    rp_state: Option<&str>,
6743) -> HttpResponse {
6744    let allow_list = match client_id {
6745        Some(client_id) => state
6746            .oauth2_client_repo
6747            .get_by_client_id(tenant_id, client_id)
6748            .await
6749            .map(|c| c.post_logout_redirect_uris)
6750            .unwrap_or_default(),
6751        None => Vec::new(),
6752    };
6753
6754    match axiam_oauth2::logout::resolve_post_logout_redirect(
6755        post_logout_redirect_uri,
6756        &allow_list,
6757        rp_state,
6758    ) {
6759        axiam_oauth2::logout::LogoutOutcome::Redirect { uri, state: st } => {
6760            let location = match (url::Url::parse(&uri), st) {
6761                (Ok(mut url), Some(st)) => {
6762                    url.query_pairs_mut().append_pair("state", &st);
6763                    url.to_string()
6764                }
6765                (Ok(url), None) => url.to_string(),
6766                // An allow-listed entry that will not parse is an operator
6767                // error, not a user-facing one; render rather than emit a
6768                // Location header we could not construct.
6769                (Err(_), _) => {
6770                    return logged_out_page(&state.auth_config, tenant_id);
6771                }
6772            };
6773            let mut response = HttpResponse::Found();
6774            response
6775                .append_header(("Location", location))
6776                .append_header(("Cache-Control", "no-store"));
6777            clear_logout_cookies(&mut response, &state.auth_config, tenant_id);
6778            response.finish()
6779        }
6780        axiam_oauth2::logout::LogoutOutcome::Rendered => {
6781            logged_out_page(&state.auth_config, tenant_id)
6782        }
6783    }
6784}
6785
6786/// Every cookie an RP-initiated logout clears: the three API cookies and every
6787/// OP-session copy a sign-in into `tenant_id` mints (T23.1.8, D-11).
6788///
6789/// W3: RP-initiated logout clears the OP browser session too — leaving it
6790/// would mean a user who logged out through one relying party is still
6791/// recognised, silently, by the next. D-11: every copy, through the one list
6792/// that minted them, so the per-tenant copy does not outlive the bare one.
6793///
6794/// The tenant is the one the logout request names. A copy minted for another
6795/// tenant is left alone: it is scoped to that tenant's path, names a session
6796/// there, and is that tenant's to end. That is the stated limit of a
6797/// browser-side guarantee; the server-side one is that a revoked row resolves
6798/// to nothing wherever its value is presented.
6799fn clear_logout_cookies(
6800    response: &mut actix_web::HttpResponseBuilder,
6801    config: &axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig,
6802    tenant_id: Uuid,
6803) {
6804    for cookie in logout_cookies(config, tenant_id) {
6805        response.cookie(cookie);
6806    }
6807}
6808
6809/// The removals [`clear_logout_cookies`] sets, as a list: the three API cookies
6810/// and every OP-session copy a sign-in into `tenant_id` mints. One list for
6811/// every logout that ends a browser's session, so the SAML single-logout
6812/// endpoint (T23.2.4, D-39) clears exactly what `end_session` does.
6813pub(crate) fn logout_cookies(
6814    config: &axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig,
6815    tenant_id: Uuid,
6816) -> Vec<actix_web::cookie::Cookie<'static>> {
6817    let mut cookies = api_logout_cookies(config);
6818    cookies.extend(crate::middleware::csrf::clear_op_session_cookies(
6819        tenant_id, config,
6820    ));
6821    cookies
6822}
6823
6824/// The three API-cookie removals of [`logout_cookies`], without the OP-session
6825/// copies: what a failed logout clears when the OP-session cookie is the
6826/// credential its retry needs (F4-R12-07).
6827pub(crate) fn api_logout_cookies(
6828    config: &axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig,
6829) -> Vec<actix_web::cookie::Cookie<'static>> {
6830    let cookie_secure = config.cookie_secure;
6831    vec![
6832        crate::middleware::csrf::clear_access_cookie(cookie_secure),
6833        crate::middleware::csrf::clear_refresh_cookie(cookie_secure),
6834        crate::middleware::csrf::clear_csrf_cookie(cookie_secure),
6835    ]
6836}
6837
6838/// AXIAM's own logged-out page.
6839///
6840/// Deliberately carries no RP-supplied content — not the `state`, not the
6841/// rejected URI. Echoing either would put an attacker-controlled string into
6842/// a page served from AXIAM's own origin.
6843///
6844/// The removal cookies carry the same attributes as the ones they clear
6845/// (`AuthConfig::cookie_secure`, D-18, for the API cookies; every OP copy for
6846/// `tenant_id`, D-11).
6847pub(crate) fn logged_out_page(
6848    config: &axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig,
6849    tenant_id: Uuid,
6850) -> HttpResponse {
6851    let mut response = HttpResponse::Ok();
6852    response
6853        .append_header(("Cache-Control", "no-store"))
6854        .content_type("text/html; charset=utf-8");
6855    clear_logout_cookies(&mut response, config, tenant_id);
6856    response.body(
6857        "<!doctype html><html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\">\
6858         <title>Signed out</title></head><body><h1>You are signed out.</h1>\
6859         </body></html>",
6860    )
6861}
6862
6863/// The answer of an RP-initiated logout whose session could not be fully
6864/// ended (D-N39, #839): `AuthService::logout` failed to revoke the refresh
6865/// tokens issued under the session or, after revoking them, to delete it, and
6866/// has logged which.
6867///
6868/// A `500`, never the relying party's redirect: following the allow-listed
6869/// `post_logout_redirect_uri` would tell the RP the user is signed out while
6870/// the session is still live. `logout` revokes before it deletes
6871/// (F4-R12-07), so every failure leaves the session in place and the request
6872/// that started the logout can be repeated to complete it ("Please try
6873/// again").
6874///
6875/// Which cookies are cleared follows from that. The API cookies always are —
6876/// this browser asked to be signed out. The OP-session cookies are cleared
6877/// only when they are not the retry's credential (`keep_op_session = false`,
6878/// `end_session` with an `id_token_hint`, whose repeat names the session
6879/// through the hint): on the cookie hop the cookie *is* what names the
6880/// session, and clearing it would turn the retry into "signed out" over a
6881/// session that is still live. Carries no RP-supplied content, as
6882/// [`logged_out_page`] does not.
6883fn logout_incomplete_page(
6884    config: &axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig,
6885    tenant_id: Uuid,
6886    keep_op_session: bool,
6887) -> HttpResponse {
6888    let mut response = HttpResponse::InternalServerError();
6889    response
6890        .append_header(("Cache-Control", "no-store"))
6891        .content_type("text/html; charset=utf-8");
6892    if keep_op_session {
6893        for cookie in api_logout_cookies(config) {
6894            response.cookie(cookie);
6895        }
6896    } else {
6897        clear_logout_cookies(&mut response, config, tenant_id);
6898    }
6899    response.body(
6900        "<!doctype html><html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\">\
6901         <title>Sign-out incomplete</title></head><body><h1>We could not finish \
6902         signing you out.</h1><p>Please try again.</p></body></html>",
6903    )
6904}
6905
6906/// Build and dispatch logout tokens for a session's participants.
6907///
6908/// Spawned rather than awaited: the user's session is gone the moment this
6909/// request returns, and making logout wait on N external HTTP calls would make
6910/// it a hostage to the least reliable RP.
6911pub(crate) async fn dispatch_backchannel_logout<C: Connection + Clone>(
6912    state: &web::Data<AppState<C>>,
6913    tenant_id: Uuid,
6914    session_id: Uuid,
6915    subject_id: Uuid,
6916    // T21.6 — the issuer the end-session request arrived under. `None` on the
6917    // deployment-wide endpoint, which is every request on a deployment with
6918    // `AXIAM__AUTH__TENANT_ISSUER_PATHS` unset.
6919    //
6920    // Back-Channel Logout 1.0 §2.4 requires the logout token's `iss` to be the
6921    // OP's issuer, and the relying party validates it against the `issuer` of
6922    // the discovery document it registered with — so a logout token minted
6923    // under the root issuer for a session established under a tenant issuer
6924    // would be dropped by every conforming RP.
6925    request_issuer: Option<&str>,
6926) {
6927    // #844 (D-N43) — the participants of the session's **lineage**, not of the
6928    // one row: a relying party that joined before a console refresh recorded
6929    // the id the refresh replaced, and was told nothing when the replacement
6930    // was logged out. An id nothing ever recorded keeps the pre-#844 scope.
6931    let lineage_id = match state.session_repo.lineage_of(tenant_id, session_id).await {
6932        Ok(lineage) => lineage.map_or(session_id, |l| l.lineage_id),
6933        Err(e) => {
6934            tracing::warn!(
6935                error = %e,
6936                "could not resolve the session lineage for logout; notifying the \
6937                 participants of the named session only"
6938            );
6939            session_id
6940        }
6941    };
6942    let participants = match state
6943        .oauth2
6944        .session_client_repo
6945        .list_for_lineage(tenant_id, lineage_id)
6946        .await
6947    {
6948        Ok(rows) => rows,
6949        Err(e) => {
6950            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "could not list session participants for logout");
6951            return;
6952        }
6953    };
6954    if participants.is_empty() {
6955        return;
6956    }
6957
6958    let mut clients = Vec::new();
6959    for client_id in participants
6960        .iter()
6961        .map(|p| p.client_id.clone())
6962        .collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>()
6963    {
6964        match state
6965            .oauth2_client_repo
6966            .get_by_client_id(tenant_id, &client_id)
6967            .await
6968        {
6969            Ok(c) => clients.push(c),
6970            // A participant whose registration cannot be loaded is dropped
6971            // from the fan-out — correct (a deleted client keeps its
6972            // participation rows), but silent until now. The RP that does not
6973            // get told sees nothing, and neither did anyone reading the logs.
6974            Err(e) => tracing::debug!(
6975                %client_id,
6976                error = %e,
6977                "back-channel logout: session participant is not a loadable client, skipping"
6978            ),
6979        }
6980    }
6981
6982    let issuer = request_issuer
6983        .unwrap_or_else(|| state.auth_config.effective_issuer())
6984        .to_string();
6985    let auth_config = state.auth_config.clone();
6986    let participant_count = participants.len();
6987
6988    // #844 — one fan-out per `sid` the lineage's clients were issued under,
6989    // because the logout token must carry the `sid` the relying party holds
6990    // (Back-Channel Logout 1.0 §2.4): for a client that joined before a
6991    // console refresh, that is the id the refresh replaced. Within one `sid`
6992    // each client is told once, as before. A client that joined under two
6993    // ids of the lineage holds two sessions in its own terms and is told
6994    // about both.
6995    let mut by_sid: std::collections::BTreeMap<Uuid, Vec<String>> =
6996        std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
6997    for p in participants {
6998        by_sid.entry(p.session_id).or_default().push(p.client_id);
6999    }
7000
7001    let mut deliveries = Vec::new();
7002    for (sid, participant_ids) in &by_sid {
7003        deliveries.extend(crate::backchannel_logout::select_targets(
7004        participant_ids,
7005        &clients,
7006        |client_id| {
7007            match axiam_oauth2::logout::issue_logout_token(
7008                &issuer,
7009                client_id,
7010                *sid,
7011                subject_id,
7012                &auth_config,
7013            ) {
7014                Ok(token) => Some(token),
7015                // This used to be `.ok()`. A token that cannot be minted (a
7016                // misconfigured issuer, an unusable signing key) silently
7017                // removed the client from the fan-out, so a whole deployment
7018                // could stop notifying anyone with no signal anywhere — on a
7019                // session-termination path, where "nothing happened" is
7020                // exactly the outcome an attacker wants. WARN, not DEBUG: this
7021                // one is never routine.
7022                Err(e) => {
7023                    tracing::warn!(
7024                        %client_id,
7025                        error = %e,
7026                        "back-channel logout: could not issue a logout token, client will NOT be notified"
7027                    );
7028                    None
7029                }
7030            }
7031        },
7032    ));
7033    }
7034
7035    // One line naming every stage of the funnel, so a delivery that never
7036    // happens can be localised without a code read: how many clients joined
7037    // the session, how many of those are still loadable registrations, and how
7038    // many of those actually got a token and a URI to send it to. Each drop
7039    // between those numbers has its own line above (or is the deliberate
7040    // "client registered no backchannel_logout_uri" skip in `select_targets`).
7041    tracing::debug!(
7042        participants = participant_count,
7043        clients = clients.len(),
7044        deliveries = deliveries.len(),
7045        "back-channel logout fan-out computed"
7046    );
7047
7048    // Participation records are dropped after the fan-out list is built, not
7049    // before — the list is what the fan-out iterates.
7050    let _ = state
7051        .oauth2
7052        .session_client_repo
7053        .delete_for_lineage(tenant_id, lineage_id)
7054        .await;
7055
7056    if !deliveries.is_empty() {
7057        actix_web::rt::spawn(crate::backchannel_logout::deliver_all(deliveries));
7058    }
7059}
7060
7061#[cfg(test)]
7062mod dpop_htu_tests {
7063    use actix_web::test::TestRequest;
7064    use axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig;
7065    use surrealdb::Surreal;
7066    use surrealdb::engine::local::Mem;
7067
7068    use super::*;
7069
7070    async fn state_with_issuer(issuer: &str) -> AppState<surrealdb::engine::local::Db> {
7071        let db = Surreal::new::<Mem>(()).await.expect("in-memory db");
7072        let auth_config = AuthConfig {
7073            oauth2_issuer_url: issuer.to_string(),
7074            sso_spa_origins: Vec::new(),
7075            ..AuthConfig::default()
7076        };
7077        AppState::for_test(db, auth_config)
7078    }
7079
7080    /// SEC-102. `htu` must be the server's own view of its endpoint, never the
7081    /// caller's.
7082    ///
7083    /// `HttpRequest::full_url()` — what this used to call — takes the
7084    /// authority from `ConnectionInfo`, which prefers `Forwarded`, then
7085    /// `X-Forwarded-Host`, then `Host`, and no trusted-proxy layer normalises
7086    /// any of the three here. With the authority sourced from the request, the
7087    /// caller chooses **both** sides of RFC 9449 §4.3 step 9's comparison, so
7088    /// a client phished into signing a proof for
7089    /// `https://attacker.example/oauth2/token` could have it accepted by
7090    /// sending a matching `Host`.
7091    #[actix_web::test]
7092    async fn htu_ignores_every_request_supplied_authority_header() {
7093        let state = state_with_issuer("https://id.example.test").await;
7094
7095        for header in ["Host", "X-Forwarded-Host"] {
7096            let req = TestRequest::post()
7097                .uri("/oauth2/token?tenant_id=x")
7098                .insert_header((header, "attacker.example"))
7099                .to_http_request();
7100            assert_eq!(
7101                dpop_htu(&state, &req),
7102                "https://id.example.test/oauth2/token",
7103                "the {header} header must not reach htu"
7104            );
7105        }
7106
7107        let req = TestRequest::post()
7108            .uri("/oauth2/token")
7109            .insert_header(("Forwarded", "host=attacker.example;proto=https"))
7110            .to_http_request();
7111        assert_eq!(
7112            dpop_htu(&state, &req),
7113            "https://id.example.test/oauth2/token"
7114        );
7115    }
7116
7117    /// The path still comes from the request — that is what keeps a
7118    /// `/oauth2/par` proof from being replayed at `/oauth2/token` — and the
7119    /// query string is excluded, which is exactly what RFC 9449 compares.
7120    #[actix_web::test]
7121    async fn htu_keeps_the_path_and_drops_the_query() {
7122        let state = state_with_issuer("https://id.example.test/").await;
7123
7124        let par = TestRequest::post()
7125            .uri("/oauth2/par?tenant_id=00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000")
7126            .to_http_request();
7127        assert_eq!(dpop_htu(&state, &par), "https://id.example.test/oauth2/par");
7128
7129        let token = TestRequest::post().uri("/oauth2/token").to_http_request();
7130        assert_ne!(
7131            dpop_htu(&state, &par),
7132            dpop_htu(&state, &token),
7133            "cross-endpoint replay must still be refused by the htu comparison"
7134        );
7135    }
7136}
7137
7138// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
7139// X7 G12 — request-object classification (plan §4.10)
7140// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
7141//
7142// T12.1-T12.3. Pure-function tests of the classifier, deliberately narrow:
7143// what a refusal *does* to the response (redirect vs 400) is the authorize
7144// service's business and is covered by `authorize`'s own tests and by the
7145// oauth2_conformance integration suite. What is pinned here is the
7146// classification itself, because the two error codes are what a conformance
7147// suite matches on and swapping them is invisible until a run fails.
7148#[cfg(test)]
7149mod request_object_tests {
7150    use super::*;
7151    use axiam_oauth2::par::REQUEST_URI_PREFIX;
7152
7153    /// T12.1 — `request` present ⇒ `request_not_supported`.
7154    #[test]
7155    fn t12_1_a_request_object_by_value_is_classified() {
7156        assert_eq!(
7157            classify_request_object(Some("eyJhbGciOiJub25lIn0.e30."), None),
7158            Some(RequestObject::ByValue)
7159        );
7160        assert_eq!(
7161            RequestObject::ByValue.into_error().error_code(),
7162            "request_not_supported"
7163        );
7164    }
7165
7166    /// T12.2 — a `request_uri` that is not a PAR handle ⇒
7167    /// `request_uri_not_supported`. This is the SSRF-shaped form: it would
7168    /// have the authorization server fetch a URL the request chose.
7169    #[test]
7170    fn t12_2_a_request_object_by_reference_is_classified() {
7171        for uri in [
7172            "https://attacker.example/request.jwt",
7173            "http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
7174            "file:///etc/passwd",
7175            "urn:ietf:params:oauth:not-a-request-uri:abc",
7176            "not a uri at all",
7177        ] {
7178            assert_eq!(
7179                classify_request_object(None, Some(uri)),
7180                Some(RequestObject::ByReference),
7181                "{uri:?} is not a PAR handle and must be refused as one"
7182            );
7183        }
7184        assert_eq!(
7185            RequestObject::ByReference.into_error().error_code(),
7186            "request_uri_not_supported"
7187        );
7188    }
7189
7190    /// T12.3 — a genuine PAR handle is **not** a request object. This is the
7191    /// regression that matters: classifying before the consume path must not
7192    /// break PAR, which FAPI 2.0 requires of every client.
7193    #[test]
7194    fn t12_3_a_par_handle_is_not_a_request_object() {
7195        let handle = format!("{REQUEST_URI_PREFIX}p6nS1_A2b3C4d5E6f7G8h9");
7196        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(None, Some(&handle)), None);
7197        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(None, None), None);
7198    }
7199
7200    /// When both arrive, the by-value object is the one named: it is the one
7201    /// the server would have had to read.
7202    #[test]
7203    fn a_by_value_object_is_named_before_a_by_reference_one() {
7204        assert_eq!(
7205            classify_request_object(Some("ey.jwt"), Some("https://attacker.example/r.jwt")),
7206            Some(RequestObject::ByValue)
7207        );
7208        // ...including alongside a PAR handle, which is otherwise untouched.
7209        let handle = format!("{REQUEST_URI_PREFIX}abc");
7210        assert_eq!(
7211            classify_request_object(Some("ey.jwt"), Some(&handle)),
7212            Some(RequestObject::ByValue)
7213        );
7214    }
7215
7216    /// A blank value is a client library filling in a template, not a request
7217    /// object. Refusing it would break a request that works today and carries
7218    /// nothing — the same emptiness rule the rest of this server applies.
7219    #[test]
7220    fn a_blank_parameter_is_not_a_request_object() {
7221        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(Some(""), None), None);
7222        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(Some("   "), None), None);
7223        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(None, Some("")), None);
7224        assert_eq!(classify_request_object(None, Some("  ")), None);
7225    }
7226
7227    /// The two codes are distinct and are the ones OIDC Core §3.1.2.6 spells.
7228    /// A test rather than a comment because the whole value of the change is
7229    /// that a relying party can tell the two apart.
7230    #[test]
7231    fn the_two_refusals_do_not_share_an_error_code() {
7232        let by_value = RequestObject::ByValue.into_error();
7233        let by_reference = RequestObject::ByReference.into_error();
7234        assert_ne!(by_value.error_code(), by_reference.error_code());
7235        assert_eq!(by_value.error_code(), "request_not_supported");
7236        assert_eq!(by_reference.error_code(), "request_uri_not_supported");
7237        // Neither is reported as a generic invalid_request, which is what the
7238        // pre-X7 code path produced and what the suite does not match on.
7239        for e in [by_value, by_reference] {
7240            assert_ne!(e.error_code(), "invalid_request");
7241        }
7242    }
7243}

7245// G-B (#817, D-N24) — `match_idp_hint`, the pure part of the provider-hint
7246// resolver: which of the three forms a hint takes, in which order, and what
7247// counts as ambiguous. The authorization endpoint's use of it (the effective
7248// list, the byte-identical fallback, PAR) is pinned by
7249// `tests/oauth2_idp_hint_test.rs`.
7250#[cfg(test)]
7251mod idp_hint_tests {
7252    use axiam_core::models::federation::ProviderKind;
7253
7254    use super::*;
7255
7256    fn cand(slug: Option<&'static str>, kind: ProviderKind) -> IdpHintCandidate<'static> {
7257        IdpHintCandidate {
7258            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
7259            slug,
7260            kind,
7261        }
7262    }
7263
7264    #[test]
7265    fn uuid_and_slug_resolve_as_before() {
7266        let okta = cand(Some("corp-okta"), ProviderKind::GenericOidc);
7267        let google = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7268        let list = [okta, google];
7269        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "corp-okta"), Some(okta.id));
7270        assert_eq!(
7271            match_idp_hint(&list, &google.id.to_string()),
7272            Some(google.id)
7273        );
7274        assert_eq!(
7275            match_idp_hint(&list, &google.id.simple().to_string().to_uppercase()),
7276            Some(google.id)
7277        );
7278        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "CORP-OKTA"), None, "slugs are exact");
7279        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "no-such"), None);
7280    }
7281
7282    #[test]
7283    fn a_branded_kind_name_resolves_to_the_only_provider_of_that_kind() {
7284        let okta = cand(Some("corp-okta"), ProviderKind::GenericOidc);
7285        let google = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7286        let apple = cand(None, ProviderKind::Apple);
7287        let list = [okta, google, apple];
7288        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "google"), Some(google.id));
7289        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "apple"), Some(apple.id));
7290        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "github"), None, "none offered");
7291        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "facebook"), None);
7292    }
7293
7294    #[test]
7295    fn kind_names_are_ascii_case_insensitive_and_nothing_else_is_folded() {
7296        let google = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7297        let list = [google];
7298        for hint in ["Google", "GOOGLE", "gOoGlE"] {
7299            assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, hint), Some(google.id), "{hint}");
7300        }
7301        for hint in [
7302            " google",
7303            "google ",
7304            "goo gle",
7305            "google-",
7306            "googl",
7307            "\u{ff47}oogle",
7308        ] {
7309            assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, hint), None, "{hint:?}");
7310        }
7311    }
7312
7313    #[test]
7314    fn a_kind_two_providers_share_is_ambiguous_and_ignored() {
7315        let a = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7316        let b = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7317        let apple = cand(None, ProviderKind::Apple);
7318        let list = [a, b, apple];
7319        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "google"), None);
7320        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "Google"), None);
7321        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, "apple"), Some(apple.id));
7322        assert_eq!(
7323            match_idp_hint(&list, &a.id.to_string()),
7324            Some(a.id),
7325            "the id still works"
7326        );
7327    }
7328
7329    #[test]
7330    fn a_slug_wins_over_a_kind_name_in_every_spelling() {
7331        let slugged = cand(Some("google"), ProviderKind::GenericOidc);
7332        let google = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7333        let list = [slugged, google];
7334        assert_eq!(
7335            match_idp_hint(&list, "google"),
7336            Some(slugged.id),
7337            "the operator's word for a provider is that provider"
7338        );
7339        for hint in ["Google", "GOOGLE"] {
7340            assert_eq!(
7341                match_idp_hint(&list, hint),
7342                None,
7343                "{hint}: never the other provider the same word names"
7344            );
7345        }
7346        assert_eq!(
7347            match_idp_hint(&list, &google.id.to_string()),
7348            Some(google.id)
7349        );
7350    }
7351
7352    #[test]
7353    fn the_generic_kinds_are_not_hint_names() {
7354        let okta = cand(Some("corp-okta"), ProviderKind::GenericOidc);
7355        let slugless = cand(None, ProviderKind::GenericOauth2);
7356        let saml = cand(Some("adfs"), ProviderKind::GenericSaml);
7357        let list = [okta, slugless, saml];
7358        for kind in ProviderKind::ALL.iter().filter(|k| k.uses_slug()) {
7359            assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&list, kind.as_str()), None, "{kind:?}");
7360        }
7361    }
7362
7363    #[test]
7364    fn every_branded_kind_is_accepted_by_its_wire_name() {
7365        for kind in ProviderKind::ALL.iter().filter(|k| !k.uses_slug()) {
7366            let only = cand(None, *kind);
7367            assert_eq!(
7368                match_idp_hint(&[only], kind.as_str()),
7369                Some(only.id),
7370                "{kind:?}"
7371            );
7372        }
7373    }
7374
7375    #[test]
7376    fn an_id_that_is_also_another_providers_slug_is_ambiguous() {
7377        let a = cand(None, ProviderKind::Google);
7378        let a_id = a.id.to_string();
7379        let b = IdpHintCandidate {
7380            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
7381            slug: Some(&a_id),
7382            kind: ProviderKind::GenericOidc,
7383        };
7384        assert_eq!(match_idp_hint(&[a, b], &a_id), None);
7385    }
7386}

7388#[cfg(test)]
7389mod logout_incomplete_tests {
7390    use super::*;
7391    use crate::middleware::csrf::COOKIE_OP_SESSION;
7392    use axiam_auth::config::AuthConfig;
7393
7394    fn cleared_cookie_names(response: &HttpResponse) -> Vec<String> {
7395        response.cookies().map(|c| c.name().to_owned()).collect()
7396    }
7397
7398    /// F4-R12-07: a failed logout leaves the session live, so on the cookie
7399    /// hop the OP-session cookie (its credential) survives the `500` page and
7400    /// the retry reaches the session; the API cookies are cleared either way.
7401    #[test]
7402    fn the_failure_page_keeps_the_op_session_cookie_only_when_asked() {
7403        let config = AuthConfig::default();
7404        let tenant = Uuid::new_v4();
7405
7406        let kept = logout_incomplete_page(&config, tenant, true);
7407        assert_eq!(
7408            kept.status(),
7409            actix_web::http::StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR
7410        );
7411        let kept = cleared_cookie_names(&kept);
7412        assert!(!kept.is_empty(), "the API cookies are cleared");
7413        assert!(!kept.iter().any(|n| n == COOKIE_OP_SESSION));
7414
7415        let cleared = logout_incomplete_page(&config, tenant, false);
7416        assert_eq!(
7417            cleared.status(),
7418            actix_web::http::StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR
7419        );
7420        let cleared = cleared_cookie_names(&cleared);
7421        assert!(cleared.iter().any(|n| n == COOKIE_OP_SESSION));
7422        assert!(kept.iter().all(|n| cleared.contains(n)));
7423    }
7424}
